### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
- **核心发现**：death avoidance 占 ~90% routing signal（D018 修正，从 ~99% 下调）。r=0.967, 11 tasks, 235 eps
- **论文**：EMNLP 2026 ARR short paper，DDL 2026-05-25（剩 11 天）。Framing v3 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology

Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

85)\\n- PaddingCollator.__call__() 正确传递 (line 128-136)\\n- compute_loss() 中 `inputs.pop(\\\"sample_idx\\\")` 获取，条件 `self._class_weights is not None and sample_indices is not None` 控制 (line 227)\\n- v2 (class_weights=\\\"auto\\\"): 权重 internal=0.743, det=1.527，正确计算并应用\\n- v3 (class_weights=null): 无权重，`loss = per_sample_loss.mean()`\\n\\n**class_weights 代码本身没有 bug。v2 和 v3 的 step 10 loss 一致（48.15），证明 结论：根因判定\\n\\n**\\\"数据文件不同\\\"假说被证伪（对 v3）**：v3 使用和 ablation-b 完全相同的数据（MD5 一致），但仍然异常。\\n\\n**真正的变量是训练策略的组合**：\\n1. **`upsample_minority: true`**（ablation-b 独有）：在 89.7% deterministic 的极端不平衡数据上，WeightedRandomSampler 确保 internal 样本被充分采样。没有它，模型每 batch 几乎只看到 deterministic 样本。\\n2. **`gradient_accumulation_steps: 1 vs 8`**：有效 batch 4 vs 32。较大 batch + 无上采样 = 每步看到的 internal 样本更少。\\n\\n**v2 的数据确实不同**（标签比例反转：internal 67% vs 10%，只有 4 个 task_type），但 v2 依然收敛了（eval_loss 0.49）。这说明数据差异导致训练行为不同，但不是致命问题。\\n\\n**建议下一步**：\\n- 用 ablation-b 的配置（upsample_minority=true, \"Generation-based routing eval 推翻了此前 logit-level 的 success 结论。\\n\\n**Logit-level (Trainer eval, 训练时)**: accuracy=74.5%, balanced_acc=79.1%, internal_recall=84.9% — 看起来成功。\\n**Generation-based eval**: accuracy=53.3%, balanced_acc=41.5%, internal_recall=26.7% — 实际失败。\\n\\ninternal_recall=26.7% < 30% 判定线 → conclusion 改为 negative。\\n\\n三种 thinking 模式（thinking_on, thinking_off, thinking_on+strip）结果完全一致，说明 thinking mode 不是根因。\\n\\n与 ablation-b 对比: accuracy 53.3% vs 65.4%, balanced_acc 41.5% vs 59.3%, internal_recall 26.7% vs 51.5%。全面落后。\\n\\n与 plan-002-generative-v1 同样的问题模式：logit-level 指标与 generation-level 指标严重不一致。模型在 next-token prediction (loss) 层面学会了区分路由，但在自回归生成时无法可靠地输出 [SIMULATE] 前缀。\\n\\n根因推测：causal LM 的 generation 和 teacher-forcing eval 是不同的推理模式。训练时看到完整序列可以区分路由，但生成时第一个 token 的选择（是否输出 \\\"[\\\" 开始 [SIMULATE]）不够确定。\"}, \"plan-002-generative-v1\": {\"status\": \"ANALYZING\", \"created_at\": \"2026-05-14T07:24:07.881Z\", \"updated_at\": \"notes\": \"重新评估路由指标（generation-based eval, enable_thinking=False）。结果：accuracy=70.67%（偏高因多数类偏向），balanced_accuracy=46.67%，internal_recall=16.67%（5/30），det_recall=76.67%（207/270）。两种 thinking 模式对比：thinking_on+strip 在50 samples 上 internal_recall=0/5, thinking_off 同样 0/5。模型输出两种类别（pred: 232 det / 68 internal），但路由预测与真实标签不相关。vs ablation-b internal_recall=51.5%，generative-v1 显著退步。此前 notes 声称 (class_weights=\\\"auto\\\"): 权重 internal=0.743, det=1.527，正确计算并应用\\n- v3 (class_weights=null): 无权重，`loss = per_sample_loss.mean()`\\n\\n**class_weights 代码本身没有 bug。v2 和 v3 的 step 10 loss 一致（48.15），证明 的极端不平衡数据上，WeightedRandomSampler 确保 internal 样本被充分采样。没有它，模型每 batch 几乎只看到 deterministic 样本。\\n2. **`gradient_accumulation_steps: 1 vs 8`**：有效 batch 4 vs 32。较大 batch + 无上采样 = 每步看到的 internal 样本更少。\\n\\n**v2 的数据确实不同**（标签比例反转：internal 67% vs 10%，只有 4 个 task_type），但 v2 依然收敛了（eval_loss 0.49）。这说明数据差异导致训练行为不同，但不是致命问题。\\n\\n**建议下一步**：\\n- 用 ablation-b 的配置（upsample_minority=true, 投稿目标\\nEMNLP 2026 ARR short paper (4 pages)，DDL: 2026-05-25（剩余 11 天）\\n\\n## 当前阶段\\n**Research Phase** — plan-012 全量分析完成 (11 tasks/245 eps, 核心假设确认)。plan-002 router SFT 准备中 (Qwen3-1.7B 下载)，plan-004 ablation 重启中。\\n\\n## Framing 演进\\n- v1 \\\"selective routing > unconditional\\\": **废弃** (D013)\\n- v2 \\\"safety-aware routing / death avoidance primary\\\": 过渡态 (D011)\\n- **v3 \\\"death avoidance dominates + counterfactual methodology\\\"**: 当前方向 (D013 → D018 数字校准)\\n\\n## Labeling 策略演进\\n- 1-step counterfactual: **废弃** (tie rate 98.2%, D008)\\n- Fork-based K-step: **废弃** (73% action divergence, 78h cost, D010)\\n- **Plan C (episode-level)**: 已采用\\n\\n## Death Decomposition 定稿结果（11 tasks / 245 episodes, plan-012 全量）\\n- Δ_full (det − internal, all tasks) = **+29.60**\\n- Δ_nd (det − internal, non-death episodes only) = **+2.84**\\n- Death avoidance fraction = **90.41%**\\n- Correlation: **r=0.9698, p=8.12e-07**\\n- Label 分布: det-preferred 86 / internal-preferred 9 / no-preference 150\\n- 关键例外: find-non-living-thing (n=40, 零死亡, Δ_full=+9.58, 全部为 non-death 性能差异)\\n- measure-melting-point (n=20): Δ_pr=0.05, 性能差距 ~100% 由 death avoidance 解释\\n\\n## Keywords v2 发现\\n- v1 keyword list: always-deterministic (所有 task 都匹配)\\n- v2 keyword list: always-internal (所有 task 都匹配)\\n- 结论: keyword heuristic 无法捕获 episode-level death risk signal\\n\\n## 当前进展\\n- MVP: DONE (PASS, 是**正面案例**（不是异常）：零死亡 task 上 Δ_nd=9.58 → routing 价值超越单纯 death avoidance\\n- 与 plan-011 keyword heuristic 失效互相呼应：关键词无法捕获的 signal，router 可以\\n- 论文分两层展示：overall ~90% death-dominated + per-task breakdown（展示非死亡信号存在）\\n- 数据基础：11 tasks, 245 eps, Δ_full=29.60, Δ_nd=2.84, death_fraction=90.41%, r=0.9698\\n\\n### D025: Classification head 路线宣告失败 (2026-05-14)\\nplan-002-formal v3-v6 全部退化为 all-deterministic 预测。根因组合：\\n- class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互（D023）使 internal 有效学习率≈0\\n- 去掉 class_weights（v6）后，89.7/10.3 极端不平衡仍导致退化\\n- **结论：2-class classification head 在此 imbalance 下不可用，pivot 到 generative SFT**\\n\\n### D026: 训练数据损坏确认 (2026-05-14)\\n`results/plan_c_test_labels/implicit_sft.jsonl` 标签损坏：no-preference 被映射为 internal，导致标签比例反转（internal 67% vs 正确的 10.3%）。\\n- plan-004 v2/v3/v4 用此文件训练，初始 loss=48（正常应 0.5-0.7）\\n- **唯一可用数据文件：`data/plan-004-implicit/implicit_sft.jsonl`**（MD5: c9b59e04de78, 2040 samples, 与 ablation-b 一致）\\n\\n### plan-003 baseline oracle 发现 (2026-05-14)\\nOracle routing 仅比 always-deterministic 好 2.6 分（-9.03 vs -11.64），且 96.2% episode 也选 deterministic。\\n- 说明 internal 仅在极少数 episode（~3.8%）上更优\\n- **直接支撑 C1：即使完美知道 ground truth label，optimal strategy 几乎等同于 always-deterministic**\\n- 论文 Table 1 的强力证据\\n\\n### plan-011 已关闭 (2026-05-13)\\nDirector 决策：接受 keywords v1 (always-det) + v2 (always-internal) 结果，不设计第三种 heuristic。\\n定位为论文 \\\"structural failure analysis\\\"——keyword routing 在 ScienceWorld episode-level 结构性失效。\\n\\n### plan-011 论文展示规范 (2026-05-14)\\n展示 keyword baseline 时用 match rate + precision + recall，不单独报 accuracy。v1 需标注 \\\"functionally equivalent to always-deterministic\\\"。\\n\\n### D013 framing pivot (2026-05-13)\\n核心方向：death avoidance dominates routing signal。\\n论文贡献 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology，非 router 架构。\\n\\n### 预筛最终结果 (2026-05-13, 25/25 tasks)\\n- death-dominated: 19, genuine-det: 5, internal-preferred: 1\\n- Genuine-det (DAF < 95%): measure-melting-point-unknown (59.4%), find-living-thing (89.2%), find-plant (93.5%), find-non-living-thing (0%, int-preferred)\\n\\n### 文献扫描结果 (2026-05-14)\\n12 篇新论文扫描，**无 direct competitor，novelty confirmed**。\\n\\n### 分类修正 (2026-05-14)\\ngrow-plant → no-signal。更新后：death-dom 25 / genuine-det 3 / internal-preferred 1 / no-signal 1\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n### 🔴 D023 确认: class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互是 plan-002 退化根因 (2026-05-14)\\n**pad_token_id 假说被 v5 彻底否定**。v5 加了 pad_token_id fix，loss 曲线与 v4 完全一致（差异在噪声范围）。\\n\\n**真正根因机制**（Reviewer 核实）：\\n- class_weights [4.5, 0.5625] → 含 internal 样本的微批次 loss 爆炸 (5-11)\\n- → grad_norm 100-600\\n- → 被 HF Trainer 默认 max_grad_norm=1.0 裁剪\\n- → internal 有效学习率 ≈ lr × 1/600 ≈ 3e-8（等于零）\\n- → 模型永远学不到 internal class\\n\\n**关键对照**：dryrun 无 class_weights 时 loss 正常 (0.17-1.03)、grad_norm 正常 (0.5-42)。\\n\\n**受影响实验**：v3 (class_weights + upsample 双重)、v4 (class_weights only)、v5 (class_weights + pad fix) 均退化。plan-004-v2 (implicit SFT + class_weights) 也出现 loss 异常 (avg 12.74)。\\n\\n### plan-004-ablation-b 隐式路由非退化 (2026-05-14)\\nimplicit router (generative SFT) 用同样的 class_weights auto 但未退化（internal recall=51.5%）。原因：generative loss (token-level CE) 与 classification loss 对 class_weights 的响应不同。generative 路径上 class_weights 只影响 loss 加权，不影响 logit 分布；分类头直接在 2-class logit 上加权，梯度更集中。\\n\\n### Qwen3 config.pad_token_id = None (2026-05-14)\\nQwen3 config 无 pad_token_id（设计如此）。ModelScope 与 HF 完全一致。router_sft_train.py L305 已有 `model.config.pad_token_id = tokenizer.pad_token_id` 设置，**非 v3/v4 根因**。\\n\\n### 数据量确认 (2026-05-14)\\n2040 samples 是 plan_c 正确全量（11 tasks × labeled_steps.jsonl）。dryrun 的 5880 来自不同数据源（plan_001b_labels/sft_triples.jsonl）。\\n\\n### AutoDL 网络 + HF 模型路径 (2026-05-14)\\n提交训练必须：(1) 本地模型路径，(2) source /etc/network_turbo，(3) HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache。\\n\\n### Qwen3-1.7B LoRA 训练显存限制 (2026-05-14)\\nRTX 5090 32GB：batch_size=32 OOM，batch_size=4 正常（29.8G）。\\n\\n### 僵尸 tmux session (2026-05-14)\\n清理 GPU 进程时同时 `tmux ls` 清理僵尸 sessions。\\n\\n### SIGHUP 误杀 (2026-05-14)\\ntmux kill-session → SIGHUP 波及无关进程。禁止 `pkill -f` 宽泛 pattern。\\n\\n### 进程存活判断 (2026-05-14)\\n必须 `ps -p PID`，不依赖 nvidia-smi。\\n\\n### vLLM EngineCore 孤儿进程 (2026-05-13)\\n父脚本崩溃后子进程成孤儿。已有 cleanup_gpu_processes()。\\n\\n### Episode 收集速度 (2026-05-13)\\n每 episode ~4.7 min。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4178, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

```text
- Checkpoint: `checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter`

Wait, there's a discrepancy here. The spec shows gradient_accumulation_steps=8, but the ablation-b notes explicitly state it used ga=1 with an effective batch size of 4. Let me check the plan-004-v3 notes to see which is correct.

Looking at the comparison table, ablation-b definitely used ga=1 as the default, while v2 and v3 both used ga=8. But the spec recorded `--gradient_accumulation_steps 8`, which contradicts what the ablation-b notes say. Either the spec was recorded incorrectly, or the script defaults to something different than what was actually used.

V5's hypothesis was to strictly replicate ablation-b's success conditions with ga=1, and it did use that setting explicitly. Yet v5 still failed at the generation level despite matching ablation-b's config, so there must be something else different between them beyond just the gradient accumulation steps.

The real problem is that if I can't reliably reproduce ablation-b's results, then the paper's C2 claim about learning routing signals becomes questionable. Though C2 was already framed as a secondary diagnostic probe rather than a core contribution.

For the paper, I should present ablation-b as evidence the signal can be learned despite training sensitivity, anchor the main narrative around C1 with the solid plan-012 data, and use the plan-003 end-to-end evaluation to compare the trained router against baselines. I need to report this to the Director and get workers spinning up on the GPUs.

Looking at what plan-003 actually requires: the baseline already ran on 245 episodes and showed the trained router degenerating to deterministic behavior, so I need to re-run with ablation-b as the trained condition instead.

The issue is that ablation-b uses an implicit generative approach (outputting a [SIMULATE] token), but the existing eval script was built for explicit classification heads. I need a worker to check the current plan-003 code, adapt it to handle the implicit router, and run the full end-to-end evaluation across both GPUs, while another worker trains ablation-b with a different seed in parallel.

But there's a resource conflict—if one worker is using both GPUs for eval, the other can't train simultaneously. Let me reconsider the sequencing. The eval needs Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct and potentially the ablation-b router on separate GPUs, plus the ScienceWorld environment. Since training is fast (only 2040 samples × 3 epochs, roughly 3 minutes per seed), I could run two replication trainings back-to-back on the available GPUs, then run the full eval once both are complete.

Actually, looking at the timing more carefully—training finishes in about 3 minutes based on ablation-b's history. And I'm realizing plan-003 might not need a traditional end-to-end eval at all. The baseline eval in plan-003-baseline-245eps computed baselines from existing episode data without running new episodes, using counterfactual labels for the oracle. So for the trained router, I could just iterate through each step in each episode, predict routing with ablation-b, and score the episode based on those predictions—no new ScienceWorld runs required. But I need to verify how the baseline eval actually works to confirm this approach.

Alright, my plan is to report to the Director, spawn a worker to investigate and run plan-003 with ablation-b, and spawn another worker to replicate ablation-b across different seeds. Before I draft that report though, I'm noticing a discrepancy in the specs: the config says gradient_accumulation_steps=8, but the analysis shows ablation-b used the default value of 1. I need to figure out whether the spec was incorrect or if something else is going on.

Looking at the evidence, it seems most likely that the spec was recorded wrong—someone probably copied the ga=8 flag from a previous experiment template when the actual training used the default ga=1. For replication, I should test with seed=123 using both the spec as written (ga=8) and with the corrected value (ga=1) to see which one actually matches ablation-b's behavior.

Actually, this is getting too tangled to resolve on my own. I'll have one Worker dig into the actual training logs to confirm what config ablation-b really used and replicate it with a few seeds, while another Worker starts building out the end-to-end evaluation code for plan-003 using ablation-b as the baseline. Then I can report back to the Director with solid answers.
```

```json
  "task_prompt": "## 任务：调研 plan-003 eval 代码，准备 ablation-b trained-router 条件\n\n### 背景\nplan-003 是 SimGate 端到端评估实验。baselines 已跑完（plan-003-baseline-245eps），结果：\n- always-internal: score=-39.07, death=42.9%\n- always-deterministic: score=-9.47, death=16.3%\n- oracle: score=-6.58, death=14.3%, sim_ratio=96.2%\n- random: score=-22.38, death=27.8%\n- trained_router_v6: degenerate (= always-deterministic)\n\n缺少的是：用 ablation-b（唯一有效的非退化 router）跑 trained-router 条件。\n\nablation-b 是 **implicit router**（generative SFT），不是 classification head。它是 Qwen3-1.7B + LoRA adapter，对每个 step 的 observation+action_history 生成回复。如果回复以 [SIMULATE] 开头则路由到 deterministic（simulator），否则路由到 internal。\n- Adapter 路径: `checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter`\n- 模型: Qwen3-1.7B at `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B`\n- Eval 参考: `eval_ablation_b_gen.py`（已跑过 generation-level eval，结果在 `results/ablation_b_gen_eval.json`）\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器: westb-13313\n- 工作目录: /root/autodl-tmp/simgate-modular-routing\n- Python: `source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n- Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct: /root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen/Qwen2___5-7B-Instruct/\n- 数据: results/plan_c/ 下 11 个 task 子目录，每个有 episodes.jsonl\n\n### 步骤\n\n**Phase 1: 理解现有 eval 架构**\n1. 找到 plan-003-baseline eval 脚本（搜索 `results/plan_003_eval/` 相关代码，或 `eval_baselines` 关键词）\n2. 理解 eval 如何工作：\n   - 是重新跑 ScienceWorld episodes（需要环境交互）？\n   - 还是基于已有 episode 数据做路由决策后分配分数（离线 eval）？\n3. 理解 \"trained-router\" 条件如何集成：\n   - 分类 head router 如何做预测？\n   - implicit router (ablation-b) 如何适配？\n\n**Phase 2: 评估方案**\n根据 Phase 1 的发现，确定最可行的 eval 方案：\n\n**方案 A（如果是离线 eval）**：\n- 对 245 episodes 中的每个 step，用 ablation-b 预测路由\n- 根据预测的路由分配分数（选 internal 则用 internal 轨迹的分数，选 deterministic 则用 deterministic 轨迹的分数）\n- 计算 episode 总分和 death rate\n\n**方案 B（如果需要在线交互）**：\n- 需要跑 ScienceWorld 环境\n- 每步先用 ablation-b 预测路由\n- 然后用 Qwen2.5-7B 执行选中的模式\n- 这更复杂但更真实\n\n**Phase 3: 实现**\n如果方案 A 可行（大概率，因为 baselines 也是这么做的），直接实现：\n1. 写脚本 `eval_trained_router.py`\n2. 加载 ablation-b adapter\n3. 对每个 episode 的每个 step 做路由预测\n4. 汇总结果\n5. dry-run 验证脚本可运行\n\n### 产出\n1. eval 方案说明（A 还是 B）\n2. 如果方案 A：实现好的 eval 脚本 + dry-run 验证结果\n3. 如果方案 B：实现方案 + 运行估时\n\n### 出错诊断\n- 如果找不到 eval 脚本：搜索 `plan_003` / `eval_baselines` / `eval_router` 关键词\n- ablation-b adapter 加载：`from peft import PeftModel; model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base_model, \"checkpoints/plan-004-implicit/final_adapter\")`",

simgate-modular-routing\\n\\n## 决策记录\\n\\n### C1 claim 最终措辞 (2026-05-14, D018)\\nDirector 决策：C1 改为 \\\"death avoidance accounts for ~90% of routing signal\\\"（从 ~99% 下调）。\\n- find-non-living-thing 是**正面案例**（不是异常）：零死亡 task 上 Δ_nd=9.58 → routing 价值超越单纯 death avoidance\\n- 与 plan-011 keyword heuristic 失效互相呼应：关键词无法捕获的 signal，router 可以\\n- 论文分两层展示：overall ~90% death-dominated + per-task breakdown（展示非死亡信号存在）\\n- 数据基础：11 tasks, 245 eps, Δ_full=29.60, Δ_nd=2.84, death_fraction=90.41%, r=0.9698\\n\\n### D025: Classification head 路线宣告失败 (2026-05-14)\\nplan-002-formal v3-v6 全部退化为 all-deterministic 预测。根因组合：\\n- class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互（D023）使 internal 有效学习率≈0\\n- 去掉 class_weights（v6）后，89.7/10.3 极端不平衡仍导致退化\\n- **结论：2-class classification head 在此 imbalance 下不可用，pivot 到 generative SFT**\\n\\n### D026: 训练数据损坏确认 (2026-05-14)\\n`results/plan_c_test_labels/implicit_sft.jsonl` 标签损坏：no-preference 被映射为 internal，导致标签比例反转（internal 67% vs 正确的 10.3%）。\\n- plan-004 v2/v3/v4 用此文件训练，初始 loss=48（正常应 0.5-0.7）\\n- **唯一可用数据文件：`data/plan-004-implicit/implicit_sft.jsonl`**（MD5: c9b59e04de78, 2040 samples, 与 ablation-b 一致）\\n\\n### plan-003 baseline oracle 发现 (2026-05-14)\\nOracle routing 仅比 always-deterministic 好 2.6 分（-9.03 vs -11.64），且 96.2% episode 也选 deterministic。\\n- 说明 internal 仅在极少数 episode（~3.8%）上更优\\n- **直接支撑 C1：即使完美知道 ground truth label，optimal strategy 几乎等同于 always-deterministic**\\n- 论文 Table 1 的强力证据\\n\\n### plan-011 已关闭 (2026-05-13)\\nDirector 决策：接受 keywords v1 (always-det) + v2 (always-internal) 结果，不设计第三种 heuristic。\\n定位为论文 \\\"structural failure analysis\\\"——keyword routing 在 ScienceWorld episode-level 结构性失效。\\n\\n### plan-011 论文展示规范 (2026-05-14)\\n展示 keyword baseline 时用 match rate + precision + recall，不单独报 accuracy。v1 需标注 \\\"functionally equivalent to always-deterministic\\\"。\\n\\n### D013 framing pivot (2026-05-13)\\n核心方向：death avoidance dominates routing signal。\\n论文贡献 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology，非 router 架构。\\n\\n### 预筛最终结果 (2026-05-13, 25/25 tasks)\\n- death-dominated: 19, genuine-det: 5, internal-preferred: 1\\n- Genuine-det (DAF < 95%): measure-melting-point-unknown (59.4%), find-living-thing (89.2%), find-plant (93.5%), find-non-living-thing (0%, int-preferred)\\n\\n### 文献扫描结果 (2026-05-14)\\n12 篇新论文扫描，**无 direct competitor，novelty confirmed**。\\n\\n### 分类修正 (2026-05-14)\\ngrow-plant → no-signal。更新后：death-dom 25 / genuine-det 3 / internal-preferred 1 / no-signal 1\\n\\n## 经验教训\\n\\n### 🔴 D023 确认: class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互是 plan-002 退化根因 (2026-05-14)\\n**pad_token_id 假说被 v5 彻底否定**。v5 加了 pad_token_id fix，loss 曲线与 v4 完全一致（差异在噪声范围）。\\n\\n**真正根因机制**（Reviewer 核实）：\\n- class_weights [4.5, 0.5625] → 含 internal 样本的微批次 loss 爆炸 (5-11)\\n- → grad_norm 100-600\\n- → 被 HF Trainer 默认 max_grad_norm=1.0 裁剪\\n- → internal 有效学习率 ≈ lr × 1/600 ≈ 3e-8（等于零）\\n- → 模型永远学不到 internal class\\n\\n**关键对照**：dryrun 无 class_weights 时 loss 正常 (0.17-1.03)、grad_norm 正常 (0.5-42)。\\n\\n**受影响实验**：v3 (class_weights + upsample 双重)、v4 (class_weights only)、v5 (class_weights + pad fix) 均退化。plan-004-v2 (implicit SFT + class_weights) 也出现 loss 异常 (avg 12.74)。\\n\\n### plan-004-ablation-b 隐式路由非退化 (2026-05-14)\\nimplicit router (generative SFT) 用同样的 class_weights auto 但未退化（internal recall=51.5%）。原因：generative loss (token-level CE) 与 classification loss 对 class_weights 的响应不同。generative 路径上 class_weights 只影响 loss 加权，不影响 logit 分布；分类头直接在 2-class logit 上加权，梯度更集中。\\n\\n### Qwen3 config.pad_token_id = None (2026-05-14)\\nQwen3 config 无 pad_token_id（设计如此）。ModelScope 与 HF 完全一致。router_sft_train.py L305 已有 `model.config.pad_token_id = tokenizer.pad_token_id` 设置，**非 v3/v4 根因**。\\n\\n### 数据量确认 (2026-05-14)\\n2040 samples 是 plan_c 正确全量（11 tasks × labeled_steps.jsonl）。dryrun 的 5880 来自不同数据源（plan_001b_labels/sft_triples.jsonl）。\\n\\n### AutoDL 网络 + HF 模型路径 (2026-05-14)\\n提交训练必须：(1) 本地模型路径，(2) source /etc/network_turbo，(3) HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache。\\n\\n### Qwen3-1.7B LoRA 训练显存限制 (2026-05-14)\\nRTX 5090 32GB：batch_size=32 OOM，batch_size=4 正常（29.8G）。\\n\\n### 僵尸 tmux session (2026-05-14)\\n清理 GPU 进程时同时 `tmux ls` 清理僵尸 sessions。\\n\\n### SIGHUP 误杀 (2026-05-14)\\ntmux kill-session → SIGHUP 波及无关进程。禁止 `pkill -f` 宽泛 pattern。\\n\\n### 进程存活判断 (2026-05-14)\\n必须 `ps -p PID`，不依赖 nvidia-smi。\\n\\n### vLLM EngineCore 孤儿进程 (2026-05-13)\\n父脚本崩溃后子进程成孤儿。已有 cleanup_gpu_processes()。\\n\\n### Episode 收集速度 (2026-05-13)\\n每 episode ~4.7 min。\\n\", \"total_chars\": 4178, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

per-task breakdown（展示非死亡信号存在）\n- 数据基础：11 tasks, 245 eps, Δ_full=29.60, Δ_nd=2.84, death_fraction=90.41%, r=0.9698\n\n### D025: Classification head 路线宣告失败 (2026-05-14)\nplan-002-formal v3-v6 全部退化为 all-deterministic 预测。根因组合：\n- class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互（D023）使 internal 有效学习率≈0\n- 去掉 class_weights（v6）后，89.7/10.3 极端不平衡仍导致退化\n- **结论：2-class classification head 在此 imbalance 下不可用，pivot 到 generative SFT**\n\n### D026: 训练数据损坏确认 (2026-05-14)\n`results/plan_c_test_labels/implicit_sft.jsonl` 标签损坏：no-preference 被映射为 internal，导致标签比例反转（internal 67% vs 正确的 10.3%）。\n- plan-004 v2/v3/v4 用此文件训练，初始 loss=48（正常应 0.5-0.7）\n- **唯一可用数据文件：`data/plan-004-implicit/implicit_sft.jsonl`**（MD5: c9b59e04de78, 2040 samples, 与 ablation-b 一致）\n\n### plan-003 baseline oracle 发现 (2026-05-14)\nOracle routing 仅比 always-deterministic 好 2.6 分（-9.03 vs -11.64），且 96.2% episode 也选 deterministic。\n- 说明 internal 仅在极少数 episode（~3.8%）上更优\n- **直接支撑 C1：即使完美知道 ground truth label，optimal strategy 几乎等同于 always-deterministic**\n- 论文 Table 1 的强力证据\n\n### plan-011 已关闭 (2026-05-13)\nDirector 决策：接受 keywords v1 (always-det) + v2 (always-internal) 结果，不设计第三种 heuristic。\n定位为论文 \"structural failure analysis\"——keyword routing 在 ScienceWorld episode-level 结构性失效。\n\n### plan-011 论文展示规范 (2026-05-14)\n展示 keyword baseline 时用 match rate + precision + recall，不单独报 accuracy。v1 需标注 \"functionally equivalent to always-deterministic\"。\n\n### D013 framing pivot (2026-05-13)\n核心方向：death avoidance dominates routing signal。\n论文贡献 = empirical finding + counterfactual methodology，非 router 架构。\n\n### 预筛最终结果 (2026-05-13, 25/25 tasks)\n- death-dominated: 19, genuine-det: 5, internal-preferred: 1\n- Genuine-det (DAF < 95%): measure-melting-point-unknown (59.4%), find-living-thing (89.2%), find-plant (93.5%), find-non-living-thing (0%, int-preferred)\n\n### 文献扫描结果 (2026-05-14)\n12 篇新论文扫描，**无 direct competitor，novelty confirmed**。\n\n### 分类修正 (2026-05-14)\ngrow-plant → no-signal。更新后：death-dom 25 / genuine-det 3 / internal-preferred 1 / no-signal 1\n\n## 经验教训\n\n### 🔴 Logit-generation gap 是关键评估指标 (2026-05-14)\n**Logit-level eval（Trainer eval during training）与 generation-level eval（autoregressive inference）结果可能严重不一致。** 必须用 generation-level eval 作为最终判定标准。\n- plan-004-v5: logit internal_recall=84.85%, generation internal_recall=26.7% → gap=58.15pp\n- plan-002-generative-v1: logit ~51.85%, generation 16.67% → gap=35.18pp\n- **唯一 gap=0 的模型：ablation-b** (logit=gen=51.52%)\n- **教训：任何 router 训练实验必须跑 generation-level eval 才能定论，logit-level 指标不可信**\n\n### 🔴 ablation-b spec 矛盾待解 (2026-05-14)\nablation-b spec 记录 `--gradient_accumulation_steps 8`，但 v3 数据分析表声称 ablation-b 用 ga=1（默认）。v5 用 ga=1 尝试复现失败。**两种可能**：\n1. spec 错误（Worker 提交时从模板复制了 ga=8），实际用 ga=1 → 但 v5 也用 ga=1 却失败，说明还有其他变量\n2. ablation-b 实际用 ga=8，v3 分析表错误 → v5 用 ga=1 是不同配置，不算复现失败\n**Worker 正在查训练日志确认。结论出来前不要假设任何一方正确。**\n\n### 🔴 D023 确认: class_weights + max_grad_norm 交互是 plan-002 退化根因 (2026-05-14)\n**pad_token_id 假说被 v5 彻底否定**。v5 加了 pad_token_id fix，loss 曲线与 v4 完全一致（差异在噪声范围）。\n\n**真正根因机制**（Reviewer 核实）：\n- class_weights [4.5, 0.5625] → 含 internal 样本的微批次 loss 爆炸 (5-11)\n- → grad_norm 100-600\n- → 被 HF Trainer 默认 max_grad_norm=1.0 裁剪\n- → internal 有效学习率 ≈ lr × 1/600 ≈ 3e-8（等于零）\n- → 模型永远学不到 internal class\n\n**关键对照**：dryrun 无 class_weights 时 loss 正常 (0.17-1.03)、grad_norm 正常 (0.5-42)。\n\n**受影响实验**：v3 (class_weights + upsample 双重)、v4 (class_weights only)、v5 (class_weights + pad fix) 均退化。plan-004-v2 (implicit SFT + class_weights) 也出现 loss 异常 (avg 12.74)。\n\n### plan-004-ablation-b 隐式路由非退化 (2026-05-14)\nimplicit router (generative SFT) 用同样的 class_weights auto 但未退化（internal recall=51.5%）。原因：generative loss (token-level CE) 与 classification loss 对 class_weights 的响应不同。generative 路径上 class_weights 只影响 loss 加权，不影响 logit 分布；分类头直接在 2-class logit 上加权，梯度更集中。\n\n### Qwen3 config.pad_token_id = None (2026-05-14)\nQwen3 config 无 pad_token_id（设计如此）。ModelScope 与 HF 完全一致。router_sft_train.py L305 已有 `model.config.pad_token_id = tokenizer.pad_token_id` 设置，**非 v3/v4 根因**。\n\n### 数据量确认 (2026-05-14)\n2040 samples 是 plan_c 正确全量（11 tasks × labeled_steps.jsonl）。dryrun 的 5880 来自不同数据源（plan_001b_labels/sft_triples.jsonl）。\n\n### AutoDL 网络 + HF 模型路径 (2026-05-14)\n提交训练必须：(1) 本地模型路径，(2) source /etc/network_turbo，(3) HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache。\n\n### Qwen3-1.7B LoRA 训练显存限制 (2026-05-14)\nRTX 5090 32GB：batch_size=32 OOM，batch_size=4 正常（29.8G）。\n\n### 僵尸 tmux session (2026-05-14)\n清理 GPU 进程时同时 `tmux ls` 清理僵尸 sessions。\n\n### SIGHUP 误杀 (2026-05-14)\ntmux kill-session → SIGHUP 波及无关进程。禁止 `pkill -f` 宽泛 pattern。\n\n### 进程存活判断 (2026-05-14)\n必须 `ps -p PID`，不依赖 nvidia-smi。\n\n### vLLM EngineCore 孤儿进程 (2026-05-13)\n父脚本崩溃后子进程成孤儿。已有 cleanup_gpu_processes()。\n\n### Episode 收集速度 (2026-05-13)\n每 episode ~4.7 min。\n"
}
```
